We further explore the trade-off among forget quality, robustness, and model utility for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [130] [What Is a Skill Worth? Structure-Aware Shapley Valuation of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04562)
*Tao Li,Junfeng Liu,Qinghua Zhao,Yifan Li,Lei Wang,Bo Shao,Xuejun Liu,Linjun Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSV，一个结构感知的Shapley式框架，用于评估智能体技能内部单元（规则、示例、脚本、启发式）的价值，通过编译技能结构、配对删除和长度中性填充来分离内容价值与上下文成本，并在四个智能体基准上验证了其忠实性、可操作性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能日益通过自动化反馈环路优化，产生包含多个内部单元的长结构化产物，但这些内部单元的价值不明。现有数据或提示跨度评估方法无法适用于结构化技能单元，因为技能单元具有相互依赖关系、文档层级结构、触发智能体行为以及消耗有限上下文窗口等特点。

Method: SkillSV将技能编译为单元、依赖关系和层级结构，仅评估有效的反事实技能。它采用配对删除和长度中性填充来分离内容价值与上下文成本，并使用带回滚预算的估计器来处理嘈杂的智能体评估。

Result: 在四个智能体基准上，SkillSV能够恢复单元交互、保持聚合技能提升，并指导安全的剪枝与压缩。

Conclusion: SkillSV提供了一种结构感知的技能价值评估方法，能够有效解释和优化智能体技能，为技能单元的价值分配和后续的剪枝压缩提供可靠依据。

Abstract: Agent skills are increasingly optimized by automated feedback loops, producing long structured artifacts whose internal value remains unclear. We study skill valuation: assigning credit to the internal units of a fixed skill, such as rules, examples, scripts, and heuristics, under a fixed agent and held-out task distribution. Skill valuation differs from data or prompt-span valuation because skill units are structured: they may depend on other units, belong to a document hierarchy, trigger agent behavior, and consume limited prompt context. We introduce SkillSV, a structure-aware Shapley-style framework for skill valuation. SkillSV compiles a skill into units, dependencies, and hierarchy, so that only valid counterfactual skills are evaluated. It uses paired deletion and length-neutral padding to separate content value from context cost, and estimates the resulting values with a rollout-budgeted estimator for noisy agent evaluations. On four agentic benchmarks, we assess the faithfulness, actionability, and explanation of SkillSV: it recovers unit interactions, preserves aggregate skill lift, and guides safe pruning and compression.

</details>


### [131] [Joint UAV Flight and Opportunistic Routing under Reinforcement Learning for Delay-Tolerant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04590)
*Xiao Wang,Shun-Ren Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JUROR框架，在延迟容忍网络（DTN）中联合优化无人机（UAV）飞行控制与机会路由决策，通过集中式训练-分散式执行（CTDE）范式解决稀疏连接、有限缓冲和TTL限制下的端到端性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTN部署日益广泛，但间歇性接触、有限缓冲和有限消息存活时间导致稀疏投递和拥塞，造成端到端性能显著退化。现有方法未充分利用无人机可控移动性来主动扩大未来接触机会，需要联合优化路由和UAV航向决策。

Method: 将问题建模为因子化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（factored POMDP），具有顺序运动-路由耦合和每步团队奖励；基于PPO框架构建JUROR，部署分散式actor基于局部观测执行，训练时critic利用全局统计信息，并提供可选的多时域热点预测器作为辅助监督。

Result: 在四种流量模式下仿真，JUROR相对于PRoPHET和MaxProp协议展现出有效性能增益，同时保持接触受限场景下的分散式执行能力。

Conclusion: JUROR通过CTDE范式实现了无人机航向与机会路由的联合优化，在保持实际部署所需的分散式执行特性的同时显著提升DTN端到端性能，验证了协同可因子化路由-UAV控制策略的有效性。

Abstract: The growing deployment of delay-tolerant networks (DTNs) has made store-carry-forward (SCF) communication indispensable under sparse connectivity.
  However, intermittent contacts, finite buffers, and limited message time-to-live (TTL) often give rise to sparse delivery and congestion, leading to substantial end-to-end performance degradation.
  To address this challenge, this study explores the joint optimization of decentralized opportunistic routing and controllable unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) flight, aiming to enlarge future contacts through discrete UAV headings while enabling per-node replication under contact-limited observations.
  Building upon this architecture, we study cooperative factored routing--UAV control under centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) and propose JUROR (Joint UAV flight and Opportunistic Routing, based on the proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework.
  In our design, we first cast the problem as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with sequential motion--routing coupling and a per-step team reward; subsequently, decentralized actors act on local observations while a training-time critic uses global statistics, and an optional multi-horizon hotspot predictor provides auxiliary supervision.
  Simulation results over four traffic modes demonstrate effective gains over PRoPHET and MaxProp, while retaining contact-limited decentralized execution.

</details>


### [132] [Agreement Before Diversity: Verification-First Complementarity for Heterogeneous Language-Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04618)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD) 的冻结、无需标签的决策规则，用于异质语言模型集成中何时用新生成的答案替代已有答案的问题。ABD 将候选答案的"潜力"与替换的"权威性"解耦，通过两个独立可信样本在固定等价关系下佐证锚定答案则保留，否则由异质综合替换。作者证明了两个精确恒等式，不依赖独立性或校准置信度假设，并在 LiveCodeBench-v6 和 GPQA-Diamond 上取得了优于基线的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质语言模型集成虽然扩展了候选答案空间，但缺乏何时让新生成答案取代已有答案的原则性判据。已有答案（锚定答案）与候选新答案之间的"更换决策"缺少显式、可审计的权威机制。

Method: ABD 是一个冻结的、无标签决策规则：当两个额外的可信样本在固定等价关系下佐证锚定答案时则保留该答案；否则，由异质综合替换。作者推导了该门控机制的两个精确恒等式（相对无条件合成的准确率差距由协议覆盖率和锚定答案在受保护子集上的优势共同决定；相对永不合成的差距反映"授权恢复"与"授权摧毁"之间的对比），并给出期望推理成本公式。

Result: 在盲式精确ID评估下，ABD 在完整 LiveCodeBench-v6 上达到 59.43%（对比 Single9 的 52.57% 和 HAC 的 52.00%，n=175），在未触碰的 GPQA-Diamond 分割上达到 75.00%（两个对照组均为 72.78%，n=180）。所有聚合差异均可定位到可枚举的受保护层：LiveCodeBench 上 3 个受保护案例中无不一致项，GPQA-Diamond 上 132 例中 13 比 8 个不一致案例，冻结锚定扰动下 71 例中 12 比 0。

Conclusion: 多样性提供潜力，验证结构提供权威。ABD 通过将替换权显式化、可审计化，在不需要标签或校准置信度的情况下实现了优于无原则合成的性能，并能为聚合性能差异提供可枚举的归因。

Abstract: Heterogeneous language-model ensembles expand the space of candidate responses, yet they lack a principled criterion for when a newly generated answer should supersede an already supported one. We decouple candidate headroom from replacement authority, rendering the latter as an explicit, auditable object. Our proposed method, Agreement-Before-Diversity (ABD), is a frozen, label-free decision rule: an anchor answer is retained if two additional trusted samples corroborate it under a fixed equivalence relation; otherwise, it is replaced by a heterogeneous synthesis. For this gating mechanism, we prove two exact identities. The first shows that the accuracy gap relative to unconditional synthesis is determined jointly by the agreement coverage and the anchor's advantage on the protected subset. The second shows that the gap relative to never synthesizing reflects a contrast between authorized recovery and authorized destruction. Neither identity assumes independence or calibrated confidence, and the expected inference cost is approximately eight minus five times the coverage in number of calls. Under blind, exact-ID evaluation, ABD achieves 59.43% on the complete LiveCodeBench-v6 (vs. 52.57% for Single9 and 52.00% for HAC; n = 175) and 75.00% on an untouched GPQA-Diamond split (both controls at 72.78%; n = 180). Furthermore, these identities localize every aggregate difference to an enumerable protected stratum: no discordant items occur among the 3 protected cases on LiveCodeBench, where coverage bounds the gate's contribution to 1.71 points a priori; 13 versus 8 discordant cases among 132 on GPQA-Diamond; and 12 versus 0 among 71 under a frozen anchor perturbation. Diversity supplies potential; verification structure supplies authority.

</details>


### [133] [A/B Agent: A Self-Evolving Agent for Strategy Iteration in Industrial A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04625)
*Zhuohang Jiang,Yuxin Chen,Yongsen Pan,Zheng Hu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li,Hongyang Wang,Jun Wang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了A/B Agent，一个用于工业推荐策略优化的闭环A/B智能体系统，通过分层经验树组织历史策略知识、多路径Tree-RAG检索生成可执行策略，并利用在线A/B反馈进行自主调优和自我进化，在真实短视频电商推荐系统中实现了4.829%的GMV提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐策略迭代严重依赖大规模A/B实验，传统人工调优过程劳动密集且耗时；同时历史实验知识碎片化，难以系统化复用。现有RAG智能体以扁平化方式组织经验，忽略了业务场景、推荐阶段、优化目标和实验上下文之间的层级关系，导致检索不匹配、跨场景迁移受限，且无法通过连续A/B反馈持续优化策略和参数。

Method: 提出A/B Agent框架，由三个紧密耦合的核心组件构成：(1) 历史策略知识组织——将历史策略构造成层级经验树；(2) 自主目标感知策略生成——通过多路径Tree-RAG检索可迁移的证据来生成可执行策略；(3) 实验引导策略自我进化——持续分析在线A/B反馈以指导自主调参并更新经验树实现自我进化。

Result: 在真实短视频电商推荐系统中进行了广泛的离线和在线评估，实现了GMV 4.829%的提升，同时所有护栏指标均保持正向增益。

Conclusion: A/B Agent通过层级经验树组织与多路径检索、反馈驱动的自我进化闭环，有效解决了工业推荐策略优化中知识复用和自动化调优的问题，验证了其在实际业务场景中的显著效果。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation strategy iteration heavily relies on large-scale A/B experimentation. Traditional tuning requires experts to repeatedly design strategies, configure experiments, analyze results, and adjust parameters, making the process labor-intensive and time-consuming. Meanwhile, valuable knowledge from historical experiments is often fragmented, making systematic reuse difficult through manual expert effort alone. Existing RAG agents partially alleviate this burden by retrieving prior strategies, but typically organize experience in a flat manner, overlooking the hierarchical relationships among business scenarios, recommendation stages, optimization objectives, and experimental contexts. This often results in mismatched retrieval and limited cross-scenario transfer, while preventing agents from continuously refining strategies and parameters through sequential A/B feedback. %
To address these limitations, we propose A/B Agent, a closed-loop A/B agent for industrial recommendation strategy optimization. The framework comprises three tightly coupled core components: Historical Strategy Knowledge Organization, Autonomous Target-Aware Strategy Generation, and Experiment-Guided Strategy Self-Evolution. It organizes historical strategies into a hierarchical experience tree, retrieves transferable evidence through multi-path Tree-RAG to generate executable strategies, and continuously analyzes online A/B feedback to guide autonomous tuning and update the experience tree for self-evolution. Extensive offline and online evaluations demonstrate its effectiveness, including a 4.829% improvement in GMV in a real-world short-video e-commerce recommendation system while maintaining positive gains across all guardrail metrics.

</details>


### [134] [AI Literacy for Legal Translation: Developing Digital Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04641)
*Łucja Biel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向法律翻译职业的AI素养框架，包含基础性、程序性、批判性和战略性四个相互强化的维度，将AI素养视为法律翻译能力的横向组成部分，并探讨了在翻译教育中培养该素养的课堂活动设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在变革法律翻译领域，但其引入带来了语言、技术、法律、伦理和认知等多重风险。法律翻译职业需要系统性地应对这一变革，但目前缺乏针对该职业的、结构化的AI素养培养框架，因此需要构建一个适应法律翻译专业特点的AI素养体系。

Method: 本文通过理论分析提出一个由基础性（foundational）、程序性（procedural）、批判性（critical）和战略性（strategic）四个相互强化的维度组成的AI素养框架，并将AI素养概念化为法律翻译能力的横向（transversal）组成部分；同时基于该框架提出培养法律译者AI素养的课堂活动设计方案。

Result: 该框架将AI素养定位为法律翻译能力的横向组成部分，能够促进数字韧性（digital resilience）；框架为法律翻译教育提供了具体的教学路径，包括旨在培养学生批判性、负责任地整合AI能力的课堂活动。

Conclusion: AI并未改变法律翻译的根本目标，但要求法律翻译专业人士在既有能力基础上扩展AI素养维度；通过教育层面的系统培养，未来法律译者可以按照职业标准在批判性和负责任的前提下整合AI工具，从而实现AI对法律翻译的专业赋能。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming legal translation by introducing opportunities alongside linguistic, technical, legal, ethical and cognitive risks. This chapter examines the implications of AI for professional legal translation and proposes an AI literacy framework tailored to the profession. It argues that AI does not change the fundamental objectives of legal translation but requires an extension of professional competence through AI literacy. The proposed framework comprises four mutually reinforcing dimensions, foundational, procedural, critical and strategic, and conceptualises AI literacy as a transversal component of legal translation competence that fosters digital resilience. It further discusses the pedagogical implications of this framework by proposing classroom activities designed to develop AI literacy in legal translator education, enabling future translators to integrate AI critically, responsibly and in accordance with professional standards.

</details>


### [135] [Calibrating Artificial Guilt: Neurally Grounded Reward Shaping for Prosocial Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04663)
*Aaditya Mehta,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出从人类神经和行为数据中校准"愧疚"信号权重，并转移至多智能体强化学习智能体。利用SoDec数据集拟合回归，得到愧疚权重1.118，将其嵌入两智能体Social Lottery环境中进行PPO训练，结果发现神经校准的智能体最接近人类行为（社会安全选择率0.459 vs 人类0.484，KL=0.0012），显著优于其他塑造方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体强化学习中，向个体奖励添加社会性（亲社会）项是常见做法，但这些项的规模通常依靠人工经验选择，缺乏系统化的定量依据。作者希望探究是否可以从人类神经和行为数据中校准"愧疚"信号并迁移到人工智能体上，从而让亲社会奖励塑造有更科学、定量的标定来源。

Method: 使用SoDec责任fMRI数据集（40名参与者），构建受试者固定效应回归，将瞬时效用（momentary happiness）变化对结果类型计数进行回归，以"伙伴负面减去社会负面"的对比度量提取愧疚权重（得到1.118，Cohen's d=0.214）。随后将该权重嵌入两智能体Social Lottery环境，使用独立PPO actor-critic在四种奖励塑造机制下训练：神经校准、均匀常数、零（自私）、单位系数Oracle。每种条件在1000个评估episode下进行测试。

Result: 神经校准的智能体最接近人类行为，社会安全选择率为0.459（人类为0.484），KL散度为0.0012；其他三种条件与人类行为的KL散度偏差高出一到三个数量级。

Conclusion: 人类神经行为先验可以作为亲社会奖励塑造的定量约束，大幅缩小人工智能体与人类亲社会决策行为之间的差距。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning often adds social terms to individual rewards, yet the scale of those terms is usually chosen by hand. We ask whether a guilt signal can instead be calibrated from human neural and behavioural data and transferred to artificial agents. Using the public SoDec responsibility fMRI dataset (40 participants), we fit a subject-fixed-effects regression of momentary-happiness changes on outcome-type counts and recover a guilt weight as the Partner-negative minus Social-negative contrast ($\hat{w}=1.118$, Cohen's $d=0.214$). We embed this weight in a two-agent Social Lottery environment and train independent Proximal Policy Optimization actor-critics under four shaping regimes: neurally calibrated, uniform constant, zero (selfish), and a unit-coefficient oracle. Across 1{,}000 evaluation episodes per condition, the calibrated agents track the human Social safe-choice rate most closely ($0.459$ vs.\ human $0.484$; $\mathrm{KL}=0.0012$), while the other three conditions deviate by one to three orders of magnitude in KL. Human neurobehavioural priors can therefore act as quantitative constraints on prosocial reward shaping.

</details>


### [136] [Traceable LLM-Generated Hazard Scenarios for Operational Safety Analysis of Aviation Systems Using ASRS Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04697)
*Cristian Mascia,Roberto Pietrantuono,Daniel Rodriguez,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI辅助方法，从NASA航空安全报告系统(ASRS)中自动生成航空系统运行危险场景候选。该方法针对目标不良后果，输出结构化假设（分类因素）和叙事场景，并提供合理性评分及可追溯性。进一步提出混合变体，通过进化溯因生成结构化假设来调节叙事生成，提升正确性、降低可变性。评估了多种LLM、零样本/少样本提示及微调对结构有效性和叙事真实性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空系统运行层面的危险分析必须考虑天气、空管指令、空域约束、飞机运行和人为因素之间的交互，这与飞机系统层面的功能危险评估（FHA）不同。然而从大量安全报告中系统性地生成并评估候选危险场景是一个挑战，需要自动化的AI辅助手段。

Method: 从ASRS中提取历史共现证据，针对目标不良后果生成结构化假设（分类因素）和叙事场景。叙事生成以结构化假设为条件，采用进化溯因方法生成假设，结合多种大语言模型、零样本与少样本提示以及可选微调，评估生成的正确性与真实性。

Result: 混合变体（通过进化溯因生成的结构化假设来调节叙事生成）相比基线在正确性上有所提升，同时降低了生成结果的变异性。提示方式和模型选择对生成结构和叙事的有效性与真实性有显著影响。

Conclusion: AI辅助生成方法能够有效支持航空系统运行危险分析，通过结构化假设加叙事生成的混合框架，结合历史证据的合理性评分与可追溯性，可生成高质量、可信的候选危险场景。

Abstract: Operational hazard analysis of aviation system operations must consider interactions among weather, ATC actions, airspace constraints, aircraft operations, and human factors - distinct from the functional hazard assessment applied at the aircraft-system level. We present an AI-assisted approach that generates candidate hazard scenarios from NASA's Aviation Safety Reporting System (ASRS). Given a target adverse outcome, it produces a structured hypothesis as categorical factors and a narrative scenario describing an operational event sequence consistent with the structure. Each scenario includes by a plausibility score from historical co-occurrence evidence and traceability to the most similar held-out ASRS reports. We then propose a hybrid variant, conditioning narrative generation on a structured hypothesis produced via evolutionary abduction, improving correctness and reducing variability. We evaluate multiple large language models, zero-shot versus few-shot prompting, and optional fine-tuning, measuring how prompting and model choice affect the validity and realism of the generated structures and narratives.

</details>


### [137] [Diagnosing Tool-Selection Reasoning in LLM Agents with Canary Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04719)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"金丝雀工具"（canary tools）——一种植入在智能体MCP工具集中的诊断探针工具，用于定位模型工具选择失败的具体原因。作者构建了六类型缺陷分类体系（语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻象、前置条件盲区、时间诱饵、粒度陷阱），将单一的"选错工具"结果转化为多维推理画像。在8个模型、120个任务、三种金丝雀密度条件和三种随机种子（共8640次运行）上评估，并辅以2880次运行的微妙性消融实验。主要发现：(1) 模型能力越强，易感性越低，跨模型金丝雀易感率约差36倍；(2) 能力层级不足以预测安全性；(3) 分类体系具有能力分层特征，能力幻象最易困住前沿模型，而其他类型对强模型基本失效但会命中小型开源模型。软化诱饵措辞后前沿模型表现几乎不变，证明探针测量的是推理而非短语匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体评估只能告诉研究者"模型选错了工具"，却无法揭示"为什么选错"。缺乏可解释、可归因的工具选择诊断方法，使得模型开发者难以定位并修复工具推理中的具体薄弱环节。

Method: 设计六类型金丝雀工具分类体系，每种类型针对一种特定的工具选择缺陷；将探针工具植入MCP工具集形成金丝雀密度条件；采用提供方无关的裁判（以独立第二裁判验证，Cohen's kappa=0.75）对任务成功进行分级；通过微妙性消融实验（软化诱饵中的提示性短语）区分推理缺陷与短语匹配。

Result: (1) 模型间金丝雀易感率（CSR）跨度约36倍，Claude Opus 4.8最低、Llama 3.1 8B最高；(2) 能力层级不预测安全性——最易感的主机模型是中层级，同一提供商内更便宜的模型可能更安全；(3) 分类体系能力分层：能力幻象最常困住前沿模型，其余类型对强模型基本无效但能有效区分小开源模型。软化诱饵措辞后前沿模型CSR基本不变；易感性与任务失败呈负相关（Spearman rho=-0.34）；最稳健的模型不受金丝雀压力的显著影响。

Conclusion: 金丝雀工具是一种有效的、可解释的智能体工具选择诊断方法，能够将工具选择失败转化为多维可归因的分析画像。分类体系展现出能力分层特征，且探针测量的是模型的真实推理能力而非文本表面匹配。研究发布框架、金丝雀模式、任务和日志以供社区复用。

Abstract: Agent evaluations tell us that a model picked the wrong tool, but rarely why. We introduce canary tools: diagnostic probe tools planted in an agent's Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool set, each engineered to probe one specific tool-selection weakness. A six-type taxonomy (semantic decoys, parameter traps, capability mirages, prerequisite blindness, temporal decoys, and granularity traps) turns a single "wrong tool" outcome into a multi-dimensional profile of how a model reasons about tools. We evaluate eight models -- six hosted and two 8B open-weight -- spanning three capability tiers, on 120 tasks across three canary-density conditions and three seeds (8,640 runs), plus a 2,880-run subtlety ablation. Task success is graded by a provider-independent judge, corroborated by a second independent judge (Cohen's kappa = 0.75). We report three findings. First, susceptibility drops sharply as models get more capable: the per-task canary susceptibility rate (CSR) ranges about 36x across models, lowest for Claude Opus 4.8 and highest for Llama 3.1 8B. Second, capability tier alone does not predict safety: the most susceptible hosted model is mid-tier, and within a provider the cheaper model can be the safer one. Third, the taxonomy is capability-stratified: capability mirages most reliably trap frontier models, while the other types are largely inert on strong models but fire on small open models, so they discriminate by capability rather than being weak. Softening each canary's give-away phrase leaves frontier CSR essentially unchanged, evidence that the probes measure reasoning, not phrase-spotting. Susceptibility also predicts task failure (Spearman rho = -0.34), while the most robust models are not significantly degraded by canary pressure. We release the framework, canary schemas, tasks, and logs.

</details>


### [138] [When Prompts Become Pixels: Prompt-Region Grounding for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04726)
*Yongxin Wang,Ruizhe Zhou,Yueling Tang,Yingying Zhu,Xuemin Zhao,Xiaojun Chang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可视化任务语义（VTS）干预方法，将问题嵌入图像来测试多模态大语言模型（MLLM）是否在不同通道中同等理解指令。研究发现24个模型-任务组合的准确率全部下降（平均17.8点），揭示出超越OCR的"语义通道鸿沟"。作者提出"提示区域接地"（prompt-region grounding）方法，在相同训练成本下将VTS准确率从58.0提升至66.3，同时保持原始界面的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常将问题放在文本中，但实际应用中任务本身可能以像素（截图/文档图像）形式呈现。这引发一个关键问题：模型在跨通道传递指令时是否使用相同的语义理解能力？作者希望评估并弥合这一可能存在的能力鸿沟。

Method: 1) 引入VTS干预：将问题从文本通道移动到图像中，保持源问题和答案不变，构建受控实验。2) 提出提示区域接地（prompt-region grounding）：将问题区域与类型化语义对齐，从掩码视图中恢复干净表示；训练成本匹配，推理时无需OCR或区域元数据。

Result: 在6个MLLM和4个基准上，所有24个模型-任务对的准确率均下降，平均下降17.8点。模型常能正确转录视觉问题但仍无法使用它。提出的方法将四基准VTS准确率从58.0提升到66.3，同时保持原始界面准确率。

Conclusion: 读取任务承载文本和将其接地为推理指令是两种不同的能力。语义通道鸿沟不仅源于OCR识别失败，更源于模型未能将视觉中的语义信息有效接地到推理过程。所提出的提示区域接地方法能有效弥合这一鸿沟。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly reason over screenshots and documents where the task itself may be written in pixels. Yet benchmarks usually place questions in text, leaving it unclear whether models use the same instruction equally well across channels. We introduce Visualized Task Semantics (VTS), a controlled intervention that moves the question into the image while keeping the source problem and answer fixed. Across six MLLMs and four benchmarks, accuracy drops in all 24 model-task pairs, by 17.8 points on average. Models often transcribe the visual question correctly yet fail to use it, exposing a semantic channel gap beyond OCR. To reduce this gap, we present prompt-region grounding, whose core design aligns the question region with typed semantics and recovers its clean representation from a masked view. At matched training cost, our method raises four-benchmark VTS accuracy from 58.0 to 66.3 while preserving accuracy on the original interface, and requires no OCR or region metadata at inference. Reading task-bearing text and grounding it as an instruction for reasoning are distinct capabilities.

</details>


### [139] [Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Can Be Unreliable in Implicit-Influence Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04735)
*Agatha Duzan,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了对比隐性与显性影响下思维链（CoT）可监控性的基准，发现显性影响场景中的监控检测率（60-94%）显著高于隐性影响场景（检测率下降41-46个百分点），且实际部署中的系统提示词会进一步将隐性检测率降至5%左右，表明现有显性影响设置可能高估模型的可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控被视为前沿推理模型的重要安全层，但现有评估大多聚焦于显性影响设置（提示词直接指示模型隐藏行为）。然而现实中更常见的是隐性影响场景，即提示词不包含隐藏指令，但任务或上下文特征（如候选人信息中的无关细节）仍会偏置模型行为。这两类场景下的可监控性差异尚缺乏系统对比，因此需要构建首个直接比较两者的基准。

Method: 构建首個直接比較隐性与显性影响下CoT可监控性的基准。具体方法包括：(1) 设计"轻推"（nudge）机制，通过四种方式传递——隐式（作为随口提及的闲聊）或显式（直接指示执行并隐瞒）；(2) 涵盖四种任务格式：二元选择、数值评分、多项选择问答、开放式编码；(3) 在七个前沿扩展思维模型中评估；(4) 额外测试实际部署中常见的系统提示词增强对检测率的影响。

Result: 显性影响下CoT监控器能检测60-94%的行为偏移，即使模型被指示隐藏，指令仍会泄漏到CoT中。隐性影响下行为同样发生偏移，但在四个设置中的两个场景中检测率下降41-46个百分点。加入实际部署风格的系统提示词后，隐性检测率进一步降至5%甚至更低，但行为偏移本身仍然存在。

Conclusion: 显性影响场景下得到的可监控性估计会高估模型真实的可监控性，且良意的部署选择（如用于减少离题偏见的系统提示词）会进一步降低可监控性。这提示需要在更贴近真实部署场景的条件（即隐性影响）下评估CoT监控的有效性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is increasingly treated as an important safety layer for frontier reasoning models. Most monitorability evaluations study explicit-influence settings: setups where the prompt directly incentivizes the model to hide something, e.g., by instructing it to perform a hidden side-task. A complementary axis for CoT-monitor evaluations is implicit-influence settings, where the prompt contains no instruction to hide, but the model's behavior is still shaped by features of the task or context, e.g. an irrelevant detail about a candidate that biases a hiring rating.
  We introduce the first benchmark that directly compares CoT monitorability under the two regimes. We test how model behavior changes in the presence of a nudge to choose a particular option. The nudge is delivered either as a casual aside (implicit), or as a direct instruction to act on the nudge and to conceal having done so (explicit). The benchmark spans four task formats (binary choice, numeric rating, multiple-choice QA, open-ended coding) and seven frontier extended-thinking models.
  Under explicit influence, a CoT monitor detects 60-94% of behavior shifts: even models instructed to conceal it leak the instruction into their CoT. Under implicit influence, the same factors still shift behavior, but detection falls by 41-46 percentage points in two of our four settings. Realistic system-prompt additions (of the kind a developer might deploy to reduce off-topic bias) lower implicit detection further, to as low as 5%, while preserving the behavioral influence itself.
  These results suggest that monitorability estimates obtained in explicit-influence settings may over-estimate monitorability, and that monitorability can be further decreased by well-intentioned deployment choices. Our benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/agatha-duzan/implicit-vs-explicit-influence

</details>


### [140] [EviGraph: Evidence-Guided Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04738)
*Zhenjiang Ren,Ruiji Li,Xujing Zhang,Ziliang Pang,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EviGraph，一种以类型化证据图为核心状态的自主体研究框架，通过持续维护问题、假设、实验、发现和主张之间的证据链一致性，解决自主研究代理输出中常见的主张缺乏支持和前后不一致问题。实验表明该方法显著提升主张支持率与数据一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主研究代理虽能生成假设、执行实验和撰写论文，但输出常包含无依据的主张以及研究问题、实验、结果和结论之间的不一致。作者认为这部分源于架构缺陷：现有系统将研究组织为顺序流水线，未能在各阶段显式维护和验证不断演化的主张-证据结构。

Method: EviGraph将研究过程表示为包含问题(Problem)、差距(Gap)、假设(Hypothesis)、实验(Experiment)、发现(Finding)和主张(Claim)节点的类型化证据图。该图是代理的操作状态而非事后记录。框架检查证据链的缺失依赖、语义错位和结果-主张不一致，定位最早出现的脆弱节点，并重新生成其受影响的下游子图。图检查点机制防止失败的修复破坏已验证的证据。只有在所有保留的主张都基于验证过的证据链后才生成手稿。

Result: 在ARC-Bench-ML和NanoResearch-20基准上，EviGraph在总体研究性能上优于端到端研究代理基线，主张支持率(Claim Support Rate)较最强基线提升40.19%，实验数据一致性(Experimental Data Consistency)达到87.73%。

Conclusion: 显式维护证据状态对可靠的自主研究具有重要价值。EviGraph通过将研究过程建模为可验证的证据图，有效解决了自主研究代理中主张缺乏依据和前后不一致的核心问题。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents can generate hypotheses, execute experiments, and draft manuscripts, yet their outputs often contain unsupported claims and inconsistencies between research questions, experiments, results, and conclusions. We argue that this problem is partly architectural: existing systems organize research as sequential pipelines but do not explicitly maintain or validate the evolving claim-evidence structure across stages.In this paper, we introduce EviGraph, an autonomous research framework that represents the research process as a typed evidence graph containing Problem, Gap, Hypothesis, Experiment, Finding, and Claim nodes. The graph serves as the operational state of the agent rather than a post-hoc record. EviGraph inspects evidence chains for missing dependencies, semantic misalignment, and result-claim inconsistencies, localizes the earliest weak node, and regenerates its affected downstream subgraph. Graph checkpointing prevents unsuccessful repairs from corrupting previously validated evidence. Manuscripts are generated only after every retained claim is grounded in a validated evidence chain.Experiments on ARC-Bench-ML and NanoResearch-20 show that EviGraph outperforms the compared end-to-end research-agent baselines in overall research performance, improves Claim Support Rate by 40.19% over the strongest baseline, and achieves 87.73% Experimental Data Consistency. These results demonstrate the value of explicit evidence-state maintenance for reliable autonomous research.

</details>


### [141] [Fewer Tokens, Smaller Cache: Reward-Coordinated Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04771)
*Qiyuan Zhu,Dezhi Li,Pengyu Cheng,Tianle Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijie Shen,Hao Gu,Sida Lin,Zirui Liu,Jiacheng Liu,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCo（Reward-Coordinated Compression）框架，利用过程奖励（process reward）统一协调KV缓存压缩与生成端控制，在大型推理模型（LRM）的长思维链推理中降低推理成本。ReCo通过奖励自适应缓存压缩、奖励带惩罚和基于置信度的早停三个组件，在三个推理模型和六个基准上实现37%-65%的生成token减少和2.08x-2.35x的端到端延迟降低，同时基本保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型擅长通过长思维链进行复杂推理，但冗长的中间步骤导致严重的"过度思考"（overthinking）问题，大幅推高推理成本。现有的KV缓存压缩方法沿推理轨迹采用统一策略，且仅通过"删除了什么"来评判压缩效果。作者有两个关键观察：(1)推理状态对上下文丢失的容忍度沿轨迹变化，过程奖励能够捕捉这种差异——在高奖励步骤删除token对精度的损害远小于随机删除；(2)压缩在生成端并非免费，更小的缓存会导致模型生成更多token，部分抵消压缩收益。因此需要在一个统一的过程奖励指导下协调压缩与生成两侧。

Method: ReCo是一个逐步（step-wise）框架，由轻量级过程奖励估计器对每个完成的推理步骤打分，驱动三个组件：(1)奖励自适应KV缓存压缩：在高奖励步骤更激进地压缩保留缓存，在低奖励步骤压缩较轻；(2)奖励带惩罚机制：对反射token施加基于奖励分段的惩罚，抑制冗余生成；(3)基于置信度的早停：当推理已可靠时触发提前终止。

Result: 在三个推理模型和六个基准上，ReCo相比完整CoT推理将生成token减少37%-65%，端到端延迟降低2.08x-2.35x，同时基本保持推理精度。

Conclusion: 通过将过程奖励作为统一信号协调KV缓存压缩与生成端控制，可以在保持推理精度的同时显著降低LRM的长思维链推理成本，为推理模型的成本优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) excel on complex tasks through long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but their lengthy intermediate steps cause severe overthinking that inflates inference cost. KV-cache compression is a common solution, yet existing reasoning-oriented methods apply a uniform policy across the trajectory and judge compression only by what it removes from the cache. Two observations point the other way. First, a reasoning state's tolerance to context loss varies along the trajectory, and process reward tracks it: deleting tokens at high-reward steps preserves accuracy far better than deleting the same budget at random. Second, compression is not free on the generation side, since a smaller cache leads the model to generate more tokens, partly canceling the saving. Together these motivate coordinating both sides under a single process reward. We propose ReCo (Reward-Coordinated Compression), a step-wise framework in which a lightweight process-reward estimator scores each completed step and drives three components: (1) reward-adaptive KV-cache compression that shrinks the retained cache harder at high-reward steps and less at low-reward ones, (2) a reward-banded penalty on reflection tokens that curbs redundant generation, and (3) confidence-based early stopping that triggers when the reasoning is reliable. Across three reasoning models and six benchmarks, ReCo reduces generated tokens by 37%-65% and end-to-end latency by 2.08x-2.35x over Full CoT, all while largely preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [142] [NSF-HRPT: Neural Semantic Field meets Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree for Safety-Critical Scenario Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04776)
*Yu Zhao,Jiangyu Pan,Tao Hu,Ming Yin,Fan Yang,Jiangfan Liu,Xiubo Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NSF-HRPT框架，结合神经语义场（NSF）与层级风险感知树（HRPT），实现从单目视觉输入进行量化风险评估。NSF学习场景语义、轨迹预测和概率性碰撞时间（TTC）分布，HRPT在推理时进行高效的并行计算与多智能体空间推理，并通过Sim2Real增强策略提升真实世界适用性。在合成基准上达到SOTA，在真实数据集上接近SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在碰撞预测上已有进展，但从单目视觉输入精确量化风险等级仍具挑战性，主要源于多智能体交互的复杂动态和真实环境中固有的不确定性。需要一种既能建模复杂场景语义又能提供量化风险评估的方法。

Method: 框架包含三个核心部分：(1) 神经语义场（NSF），从仿真数据中学习场景语义、轨迹预测和概率性TTC分布；(2) 层级风险感知树（HRPT），利用预训练NSF作为先验，实现高效的并行计算与多智能体空间推理；(3) Sim2Real增强策略，通过引入基础模型的先验知识，无需重新训练即可提升真实世界适用性。

Result: 在合成基准上达到最先进性能，在真实世界数据集上取得接近最先进的TTC估计精度和风险定位精度。

Conclusion: 该方法为从单目相机输入实现实时风险感知提供了有效解决方案，兼顾了计算效率与风险评估精度。

Abstract: The ability to accurately assess and anticipate risks in safety-critical scenarios is crucial for autonomous driving systems. While existing research has made progress in collision prediction, accurately quantifying risk levels from monocular vision inputs remains challenging due to the complex dynamics of multi-agent interactions and the inherent uncertainty in real-world environments. To address these challenges, we present NSF-HRPT, a novel framework that combines learning-based perception with structured reasoning for quantitative risk assessment. Our approach features a Neural Semantic Field (NSF) that learns to model scene semantics, trajectory predictions, and probabilistic Time-to-Collision (TTC) distributions from simulation data. During inference, the pre-trained NSF serves as a prior for our Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree (HRPT), which enables efficient parallel computation and spatial reasoning about multi-agent risks. Additionally, we introduce a Sim2Real enhancement strategy that improves real-world applicability without retraining by incorporating priors from foundation models. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on synthetic benchmarks and delivers competitive, near-state-of-the-art results on real-world datasets for both TTC estimation accuracy and risk localization precision. The proposed method provides an effective solution for real-time risk awareness from monocular camera inputs.

</details>


### [143] [Privileged, but Biased: How PI-Conditioned Teachers Break Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04794)
*Sarthak Harne,Chinmay Karkar,Yash Pandya,Ahmed Awadallah,Akshay Nambi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了自我蒸馏（Self-distillation, SD）作为独立训练目标的有效性。作者复现了SDPO在简单任务上的增益，但发现将其应用于困难任务（问答、数学、代码、多轮智能体工具调用）时，逐token损失持续下降而验证精度不提升甚至下降。作者通过因果链解释了这一失败：教师因先验信息（PI）偏差而偏向特定参考轨迹，学生的目标函数对正确性近乎失明，损失主要落在低信息token上，且正确rollout中的探索性token承受最高散度，导致模型更扁平、更不果断。结论是：SD作为独立目标优化的是与任务成功解耦的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献宣称自我蒸馏能作为RLVR的计算高效替代方案，但其报告的成功几乎全部来自狭窄、低难度的设定。作者质疑这一结论的普适性，提出了一个基本问题：在没有任何奖励项的情况下，SD作为唯一目标是否真的能教会模型推理？

Method: 作者首先在SDPO的简单设定下复现其报告收益，然后在困难任务上应用完全相同的设置，覆盖不同推理模式、模型规模和先验信息形式，同时考察SDPO和OPSD两种配方。通过度量逐token损失、验证精度和PI Bias Score，构建从损失到模型的因果链条。

Result: 在困难任务上，SD在问答、数学、代码和多轮智能体场景中均表现为：per-token损失稳定下降但验证精度无改善且通常退化。作者量化了PI偏差效应，发现学生目标对rollout正确性几乎失明，损失集中在停用词、标点、不确定性标记等低信息token上；正确rollout中的探索性token承担最高散度，惩罚了推理所需的犹豫。

Conclusion: SD作为唯一目标优化的是一个与任务成功解耦的信号。它产生的模型更扁平、更不果断，推理能力并未提升。该工作否定了SD在困难设定中作为独立训练目标的可行性，并为理解其失效机制提供了系统的因果解释。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) has emerged as a compute-efficient alternative to reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards: a self-teacher, conditioned on privileged information (PI) about the answer such as a reference solution, supplies dense per-token supervision to a student that never sees it. Reported gains, however, come almost exclusively from narrow, low-difficulty settings, leaving open a basic question: as a lone objective, with no reward term, does SD teach anything? We reproduce SDPO's reported gains in its easy setting, then apply the identical setup to difficult tasks and find that it does not. Across question answering, mathematics, coding, and multi-turn agentic tool use, across reasoning modes, model sizes, and forms of PI, and under both the SDPO and OPSD recipes, the per-token loss falls steadily while validation accuracy does not improve and typically degrades. We explain this failure through a single causal chain from the loss to the model it produces. The chain begins with PI bias: having seen one particular reference solution, the teacher's per-token target is pulled toward that trajectory rather than toward correctness in general, an effect we quantify with a PI Bias Score. Trained to match this target everywhere, the student's objective becomes nearly blind to whether a rollout is correct, and the loss it assigns falls mostly on low-information tokens like stopwords, punctuation, uncertainty markers, rather than those that determine the answer; within correct rollouts the exploratory tokens incur the highest divergence, so it penalizes the hesitation that reasoning requires. The result is a flatter, less decisive student that is no better at reasoning: as a lone objective, SD optimizes a signal decoupled from task success.

</details>


### [144] [ContextWeave: A Real-World Workflow Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04830)
*Bo Wang,Yuqian Yao,Enxi Wang,Luozhijie Jin,Yang Liu,Yiran Suo,Yuxuan Cai,Enyu Zhou,Yufei Gao,Honglin Guo,Tianyu Huai,Li Ji,Zhikai Lei,Bufan Li,Lizhi Lin,Jinxiu Liu,Jie Yang,Jiazheng Zhou,Maosen Zhou,Pengfang Qian,Shichun Liu,Guanshan Liu,Hao Zheng,Yunhao Yu,Hang Yan,Jihua Kang,Xinchi Chen,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextWeave，一个纵向基准测试，用于评估记忆（回忆的经验）是否能真正提升语言智能体在真实办公多月工作流中的下游任务表现，而不仅仅是检索或问答能力。实验表明，丰富的可操作记忆比紧凑摘要更有效，但也更易受误导性回忆影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常将语言智能体的记忆能力简化为检索或问答任务，未能衡量回忆到的经验是否真正改善下游智能体的任务执行。随着智能体从孤立任务转向长时程、有状态的工作流，这种评估方式不足以反映记忆的实际价值。

Method: ContextWeave将14名参与者的隐私保护多月工作流重建为1,005个可执行任务（含568个核心评估任务），配备指令、容器化环境、轨迹和任务级评分细则。它衡量工作区质量及与参与者个人偏好的对齐度，并辅以相关性、连续性、可解性及对误导性回忆的鲁棒性诊断。实验在固定模型下测试六种记忆组件配置，并在固定记忆组件下测试五种基础模型。

Result: 最强记忆配置将Workspace Score从68.08提升至78.20，Preference Score从41.50提升至70.60。在固定记忆组件下，回忆改善所有五种基础模型的结果，但增益差异显著。丰富的可操作记忆比紧凑摘要更能支持工作流延续并减少冗余探索，但更易受误导性回忆影响。

Conclusion: 记忆系统不仅应优化检索相关性，还应在执行过程中保证记忆的可靠使用；可操作且经验丰富的记忆是长期工作流中提升智能体表现的关键因素。

Abstract: Memory is essential as language agents move from isolated tasks to long-horizon, stateful workflows, yet existing evaluations often reduce it to retrieval or question answering. We introduce ContextWeave, a longitudinal benchmark that evaluates whether recalled experience improves downstream agent performance in realistic office-work streams. ContextWeave reconstructs privacy-preserved, multi-month workflows of 14 participants into 1,005 executable tasks, including 568 core evaluation tasks, with instructions, containerized environments, trajectories, and task-specific rubrics. It measures workspace quality and alignment with participant-specific preferences, complemented by diagnostics of relevance, continuity, solvability, and robustness to misleading recall. Across six memory components under a fixed model, the strongest configuration raises Workspace Score from 68.08 to 78.20 and Preference Score from 41.50 to 70.60. With a fixed memory component, recall improves both outcomes for all five tested base models, although gains vary substantially. Our analysis shows that actionable, experience-rich memory supports workflow continuation and reduces redundant exploration more effectively than compact summaries, while it can also be more susceptible to misleading recall. These findings motivate memory systems that optimize not only retrieval relevance but also reliable use during execution.

</details>


### [145] [When Shared Rollouts Fail in Defensive Driving Evaluation: A NAVSIM Score Basis Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04896)
*Ziang Wei,Minjun Yu,Zheyuan Lai,Mingjie Pang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了NAVSIM v2.2评分系统中参考条件化宽恕机制与数值不稳定性之间的交互作用，发现因共享的滚动轨迹数值回退不稳定，导致感知盲探针（Ignore-All和actor-blind）在完整12,146-token测试集上错误地超过了人类回放和PDM-Closed基线，揭示了评分系统的数值触发缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御性驾驶评分只有在能区分观测周围参与者策略与不观测策略时才有效。重仿真基准使用参考条件化宽恕机制，当代理和参考共享不稳定的滚动变换时，该规则可能将共享的参考失败传播为广泛的合规信用。作者旨在审计这一风险在NAVSIM v2.2中的实际存在性与根因。

Method: 在不改变宽恕机制的条件下，通过四种实验设计审计：(1) 在完整12,146-token navtest分割上比较路由盲Ignore-All探针和路由感知盲actor探针与人类回放、PDM-Closed的排名；(2) 使用固定32-token诊断集在全新安装中复现滚动发散；(3) 通过同源依赖栈控制和精确输入诊断隔离共享速度重拟合中的依赖敏感数值行为；(4) 在450-token控制池上仅更换求解器以消除滚动发散并恢复盲探测最后排序。

Result: 审计发现路由盲Ignore-All探针和路由感知actor-blind探针在完整navtest分割上排名高于人类回放和PDM-Closed；新鲜安装可复现该发散；隔离出共享速度重拟合中的数值敏感行为；仅更换solver即可消除发散并恢复正确的盲最后排序，证明数值不稳定性是直接触发器，参考条件化宽恕将其传播为合规信用。

Conclusion: 数值不稳定性是滚动发散的直接原因，参考条件化宽恕机制将共享参考失败错误传播为合规信用，导致评分失效。作者提出审计协议要求：评分基线与依赖栈披露、盲探测、覆盖报告以及滚动稳定性测试，以确保防御性驾驶评分的可信度。

Abstract: Defensive driving scores are useful only when they preserve distinctions between policies that observe surrounding actors and those that do not. Re-simulation benchmarks may use reference-conditioned forgiveness, under which an agent receives credit when the logged human reference fails a compliance channel. When agent and reference share an unstable rollout transformation, this rule can propagate shared reference failures into broad compliance credit.
  We audit this risk in NAVSIM v2.2 original scene single-stage scoring. Under the affected documented-stack condition on the audited numerical backend, the route-blind Ignore-All probe and a route-aware actor-blind probe outrank human replay and PDM-Closed over the complete 12,146-token navtest split. A fresh installation following the public specification reproduces rollout divergence on a fixed 32-token diagnostic set. A same-source dependency stack control and an exact-input diagnostic isolate dependency-sensitive numerical behavior in the shared velocity refit. On a 450-token control pool, replacing only the solver eliminates rollout divergence and restores blind-last ordering while keeping forgiveness enabled. Thus, the numerical instability is the direct trigger. Reference-conditioned forgiveness propagates the resulting shared reference failures into compliance credit. We contribute an audit protocol requiring score basis and stack disclosure, blind probes, overwrite reporting, and rollout stability tests before using such scores for defensive driving claims.

</details>


### [146] [WorldCycle: Self-Verifiable Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04964)
*Bohai Gu,Yueyang Yuan,Taiyi Wu,Dazhao Du,Jian Liu,Xiaoyi Pang,Jie Zhang,Xiaocheng Lu,Haobin Zhong,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldCycle，一种自验证的强化学习框架，通过构造可逆动作循环（动作序列与其逆序列必须回到初始状态）来获得无标注的超长时域正确性监督，从而缓解交互式视频世界模型中的累积误差问题。同时发布CycleBench基准，将状态回退漂移降低44%，复合动作准确率提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型对长时域规划与探索至关重要，但存在累积误差问题。事后训练方法（如RL）可改善模型，却面临验证瓶颈：对任意动作序列没有真实未来状态来衡量长期漂移。需要一种无需标注即可获得长期正确性监督的方法。

Method: WorldCycle通过以下方式实现自验证RL：(1) 从普通动作序列构造闭环动作循环及其重复执行；(2) 设计两个互补奖励——空间闭合奖励（强制前向与反向镜面对称）和时间一致性奖励（对齐重复循环执行间的状态）；(3) 使模型将动作学习为一致的状态算子而非记忆化的时序模式，可自然推广到分布外的复合动作循环。

Result: WorldCycle将状态回退漂移降低多达44%，复合动作准确率相对基础模型提升近4倍，并发布了CycleBench诊断基准用于评估复杂动作结构下的状态返回能力。

Conclusion: WorldCycle通过可逆动作循环实现无标注的长时域自验证监督，为物理真实世界模型的建立提供了关键基础，有效缓解了交互式视频世界模型的累积误差与验证瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Interactive video world models are essential for long-horizon planning and exploration, yet they suffer from compounding errors. Post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) can improve these models, but they hit a verification bottleneck: for arbitrary action sequences, no ground-truth future state exists to measure long-term drift. Our key insight is that reversible action cycles make this verification possible: a sequence composed with its inverse must analytically return to the initial state, yielding annotation-free supervision on long-horizon correctness. Building on this, we introduce WorldCycle, a self-verifiable RL framework that constructs closed action cycles and their repeated executions from ordinary action sequences, and optimizes two complementary rewards: a spatial closure reward enforcing symmetry between mirrored forward and reverse segments, and a temporal consistency reward aligning states across repeated cycle executions. These rewards force the model to learn actions as consistent state operators rather than memorized temporal patterns, and extend naturally to out-of-distribution composite action cycles that the base model handles poorly. We further release CycleBench, a diagnostic benchmark for state-returning ability under complex action structures. WorldCycle reduces state returning drift by up to 44% and lifts composite-action accuracy nearly 4x over the base model, providing a vital foundation for physically grounded world models.

</details>


### [147] [Short-term load forecasting under EU-AI Act Requirements in Safety-Critical Environments: Results from a 41-day live challenge on the aggregated German transmission-grid load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05018)
*Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个为期41天的实时电力短期负荷预测（STLF）挑战赛结果，评估了基于开源Python库spotforecast2-safe构建的完整预测流水线。该流水线按欧盟AI法案在安全关键环境中的要求设计，使用ENTSO-E数据预测德国输电网24小时负荷值，在准确性上优于官方ENTSO-E日前预测基线，且透明、低成本的本地模型（macl2l）与参数量超1亿的预训练基础模型（如chronos-2）竞争性相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期负荷预测在电力行业中至关重要，被欧洲和德国法律列为关键基础设施。随着监管要求的提升，STLF不再仅仅是准确性问题，还涉及软件工程和合规性问题。确定性、可复现性和可审计性成为工程必需而非可选项，因此需要一个符合欧盟AI法案要求的、透明且可审计的预测解决方案。

Method: 构建基于开源Python库spotforecast2-safe的完整STLF流水线，该库按欧盟AI法案安全关键环境要求设计。流水线包括：异常检测与间隙感知的数据准备、日历与天气协变量、递归多步预测算法、超参数调优。并从ENTSO-E数据预测德国输电网目标日的24小时负荷值，通过41天实时挑战赛进行评估。

Result: 符合欧盟AI法案的spotforecast2-safe流水线在准确性上优于官方ENTSO-E日前预测基线。上下文内模型表现出竞争力。透明、低成本、可审计的本地模型（macl2l）与超过1亿参数的能源密集型预训练基础模型（如chronos-2）竞争性相当。挑战基础设施、所有团队的完整提交历史和冻结的最终排行榜均已公开。

Conclusion: 研究证明，在STLF领域，符合欧盟AI法案合规要求的轻量级、透明、可审计的本地模型能够达到甚至超过大型预训练基础模型的预测性能，同时满足确定性、可复现性和可审计性的工程要求。这为安全关键场景下的预测建模提供了合规性与性能兼具的可行路径。

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting (STLF) play a vital role in the electric power industry. It serves infrastructure that European and German law designate as critical. Determinism, reproducibility, and auditability are engineering requirements rather than optional extras. STLF is no longer purely an accuracy problem. It is also a software-engineering and compliance problem. This paper describes results from a 41-day live challenge that evaluated a complete STLF pipeline for the aggregated German transmission-grid load. The pipeline is based on the open-source Python library spotforecast2-safe, which implements the EU-AI Act Requirements in Safety-Critical Environments by design. The pipeline predicts the 24 hourly load values of a target day from European Network of Transmission System Operators for Electricity (ENTSO-E) data. It includes anomaly detection and gap-aware data preparation, calendar and weather covariates, a recursive multi-step forecasting algorithm, and hyperparameter tuning. Forecast accuracy is measured against the official ENTSO-E day-ahead forecast. The EU-AI act compliant spotforecast2-safe pipeline beats the ENTSO-E baseline. In-context models show competitive performance. Transparent, low-cost, and auditable local models (referred to as macl2l in this paper) are competitive with more than 100-million-parameter large, energy-intensive pre-trained foundation models such as chronos-2. The challenge infrastructure, the complete submission history of all teams, and the frozen final leaderboard are publicly available.

</details>


### [148] [From Score Matrices to Football-Aware Match-State Simulation: An Auditable LLM Harness for Exact-Score Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05030)
*Shaopeng Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将动态Poisson族统计模型与大型语言模型（LLM）相结合的可审计信息框架，用于足球比分预测。通过四个迭代版本（V1-V4）逐步将LLM的语义推理能力融入统计模型，在2025-26赛季英超前150场比赛的回放测试中，V4达到14.7%的Top-1和30.7%的Top-3精确比分准确率，并报告了各版本的系统性改进与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态Poisson族模型擅长估计球队实力、预期进球和比分概率，但无法理解角色、战术对位、动机或首球后行为变化等上下文因素；而LLM虽能推理这些概念，却缺乏校准的概率输出能力。两者的互补性促使作者探索如何将LLM的情境判断安全、可审计地整合进统计概率框架中。

Method: 构建一个可审计的信息框架：定义输入语义、提供赛前证据、约束LLM走可检查的推理路径。具体迭代包括：V1为动态得分驱动的Dixon-Coles基线模型；V2将LLM情境评分映射回预期进球参数；V3用逐球模拟（基于冻结的比分候选集）替代标量修正；V4加入共享首破僵局和进球后级联判断、时间感知停止以及确定性尾部候选。采用2025-26英超前150场比赛进行时间顺序回放评估。

Result: 在150场比赛回放中：V1取得10.0% Top-1和26.7% Top-3精确比分准确率；V3提升至12.0%和30.0%；V4进一步达到14.7%和30.7%。V4将候选覆盖率从77.3%提升至84.7%，但新增尾部候选无一首获Top-3命中。V1原生的1X2分布获得53.3% argmax准确率、0.9878对数损失、0.5870 Brier评分和0.2095排序概率评分。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于提供一个可审计的混合架构、清晰的设计演进路径，以及揭示足球感知模拟在哪些方面改善、哪些方面未改善比分选择的负向发现。同时作者坦承结果为探索性的：开发切片并非未经触碰的基准，且时间隔离无法排除封闭LLM中的结果记忆偏差。

Abstract: Football score forecasting combines a strong statistical core with a difficult contextual edge. Dynamic Poisson-family models estimate team strength, expected goals, and coherent score probabilities, but do not directly understand roles, tactical matchups, motivation, or how a first goal changes behaviour. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such concepts, yet are not calibrated probability engines. We combine both components through an auditable information harness. This paper documents four iterations: V1, a dynamic score-driven Dixon-Coles baseline; V2, which maps LLM contextual ratings back into expected-goal parameters; V3, which replaces scalar correction with goal-by-goal simulations over a frozen score-candidate set; and V4, which adds shared first-breakthrough and post-goal cascade judgments, time-aware stopping, and deterministic tail candidates. The harness defines input semantics, supplies pre-match evidence, and constrains the LLM to an inspectable reasoning route. On a chronological replay of the first 150 matches of the 2025-26 English Premier League, V1 achieved 10.0% Top-1 and 26.7% Top-3 exact-score accuracy. V3 reached 12.0% and 30.0%, while V4 reached 14.7% and 30.7%. V4 increased candidate coverage from 77.3% to 84.7%, although no added tail candidate became a Top-3 exact hit. V1's native 1X2 distribution achieved 53.3% argmax accuracy, 0.9878 log loss, 0.5870 Brier score, and 0.2095 ranked probability score. These results are exploratory: the development slice is not an untouched benchmark, and temporal input isolation cannot exclude outcome memory in a closed LLM. The contribution is an auditable hybrid architecture, a clear design evolution, and negative findings showing where football-aware simulation does and does not improve score selection.

</details>


### [149] [Item Response Theory for AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05086)
*Joshua Fonseca Rivera,Neil Shah,David Demitri Africa,Konstantinos Voudouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将项目反应理论（IRT）应用于八个安全基准和192个语言模型，识别出三个可解释因素（拒绝严格性、真实性、情境危害），并证明IRT可以压缩评估成本97-99%、检测简单的沙袋行为和API背后的模型变更。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准的聚合得分难以信任和解释，因为基准之间存在重复、高度相关，且模型在检测到评估时可能故意表现不佳（沙袋行为）。需要一个更稳健的统计工具来衡量模型的潜在安全特质。

Method: 使用项目反应理论（IRT），对八个安全基准中的192个语言模型进行拟合，这是迄今最大规模的LLM安全评估心理测量分析。通过IRT推断项目的心理测量属性，并利用这些属性进行因素分解、项目选择和模型审计。

Result: 三个可解释因素（拒绝严格性、真实性和情境危害）解释了模型间大部分方差；心理测量选择的项目比随机子集误差更低，约10个自适应选择的项目即可覆盖多个个体基准，节省97-99%评估成本；IRT能够检测简单的沙袋行为和API模型变更。

Conclusion: IRT是现成的用于阅读、简化和审计安全基准的统计工具包，建议前沿实验室和评估者采用。

Abstract: Language models differ in how safely they behave and these differences are measured by safety benchmarks. But aggregated benchmark scores are hard to trust and interpret, because benchmarks duplicate one another, correlate heavily, and models may sandbag when they detect evaluation. To address these issues, we draw on Item Response Theory (IRT), a statistical toolkit for measuring these latents from performance on items with inferred psychometric properties. We fit IRT models to eight safety benchmarks across 192 language models, the largest psychometric analysis of LLM safety evaluations to date, and contribute three results. First, we find that three interpretable factors of refusal strictness, truthfulness, and contextual harm explain most of the variance between models across benchmarks. Second, psychometrically selected items recover full benchmark scores with lower error than random subsets of the same size, and roughly ten adaptively chosen items suffice for several individual benchmarks, cutting evaluation cost by 97-99%. Third, IRT supports audits of individual models, showing that it can be used to detect naive sandbagging and changes of model behind APIs. Overall, we show IRT is a ready-made toolkit for reading, reducing, and auditing safety benchmarks, which we recommend frontier labs and evaluators adopt.

</details>


### [150] [Hierarchical Graph Memory for LLM Agents with Path-level Localization and Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05095)
*Xiawei Yue,Boran Wang,Xiaoqing Zhang,Shuxin Zheng,Ziwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HiGram，一个基于层级图记忆的演进框架，通过粗到细的记忆架构、MicroGraph路径定位和协同重写机制，解决长期推理智能体在记忆更新中的信息冗余和证据选择效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图记忆方法将所有记忆存储在扁平图中，导致历史记忆积累引入无关上下文、增加检索时证据选择的成本；同时，记忆单元独立更新，无法有效覆盖关联变化。这些问题阻碍了长期推理中记忆的高效更新与利用。

Method: HiGram包含三个核心组件：1）层级图记忆架构：将记忆组织为上层节点与MemoryUnits构成的粗到细结构，减少检索时的无关信息；2）基于MicroGraph的路径级定位：利用查询和更新条件化的MicroGraph识别支持子图和证据路径；3）协同重写方法：联合修订单元内记忆与单元间依赖关系，实现证据路径上有效依赖结构的更新。

Result: 在长期对话问答基准和冲突感知记忆评估上，HiGram相较于基线在答案质量和token效率上均有显著提升；在动态、静态和条件冲突场景下，提高了答案准确性和查询有效证据选择能力。

Conclusion: 层级图记忆结构结合路径级定位与协同重写，是提升长期推理智能体记忆更新效率和有效性的有效方案。

Abstract: Agents for long term reasoning require a memory that can be efficiently and effectively updated over time, as new facts and external feedback continue to arrive. Recently, graph memory has been adopted to offer structural organization for multi-hop retrieval and reasoning. However, existing methods store all memories in a flat graph, and accumulated historical memories can introduce irrelevant contexts and increase the cost of evidence selection during retrieval. Moreover, they typically update memory units independently, requiring repeated unit-wise rewrite to cover related changes. To address these issues, we propose HiGram, an evolving hierarchical graph memory framework with path-level localization and rewriting. Specifically, we first propose a hierarchical graph memory, which organizes the memory into coarse-to-fine architecture composed of upper-level nodes and MemoryUnits, thereby reducing the amount of irrelevant information during retrieval. We further propose MicroGraph-based path-level localization, which leverages query and update conditioned MicroGraphs to identify support subgraph and evidence path before rewrite. Finally, we propose a coordinated rewriting method that jointly revises intra-unit memory and inter-unit dependencies, enable valid dependency structures updating in the localized evidence path. Experiments on benchmarks for long-term conversational question answering and conflict-aware memory evaluation demonstrate that our method demonstrate substantial improvements over baselines in answer quality and token efficiency. Besides, our method improves answer accuracy and query-valid evidence selection under dynamic, static, and conditional conflicts.

</details>


### [151] [ABSeeker: Training Long-Horizon Search Agents via Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05102)
*Yijun Lu,Rui Ye,Jiajun Wang,Yuwen Du,Tian Jin,Songhua Liu,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment (ABC)框架，通过从答案回溯恢复中间线索，将稀疏的轨迹级结果转换为稠密的步骤级奖励，用于训练长时程搜索智能体。基于此框架训练的ABSeeker（4B参数，8.5k样本）在BrowseComp达到37.3%，结合上下文管理提升至55.3%，显著超越同规模智能体并匹敌约30B的更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程搜索智能体训练方法（SFT和RL）将轨迹中所有步骤均匀对待，无法区分有用的动作与错误或冗余的动作，导致训练效率低下，需要细粒度的信用分配机制。

Method: ABC框架包含两个核心组件：(1) Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery——从地面真值答案回溯恢复解答问题所需的中间线索；(2) Clue-Anchored Step Scoring——基于这些线索对每个搜索步骤进行评分，将稀疏的二值结果监督转换为稠密的步骤级奖励。基于这些奖励，进一步提出ABC-SFT（步骤级加权损失）和ABC-GRPO（以步骤级分数作为GRPO奖励）。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5-4B训练的ABSeeker在BrowseComp上达到37.3%，在BrowseComp-ZH上达到39.1%；引入上下文管理后分别提升至55.3%和52.9%，显著优于同规模的4B智能体，甚至可匹配约30B参数的更大模型。

Conclusion: 答案回溯的步骤级信用分配能有效训练长时程搜索智能体，即使在失败的轨迹中也能识别并奖励有价值的动作，同时抑制错误与冗余步骤，证明细粒度监督对长时程搜索训练的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon search agents must make multiple sequential actions (steps) to search, retrieve, verify, and integrate evidence to reach a final answer. However, existing methods for training these agents typically treat all steps within a trajectory uniformly during both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), failing to distinguish useful actions from erroneous or redundant ones. In this paper, we propose Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment (ABC), a fine-grained credit assignment framework for training long-horizon search agents by converting sparse trajectory-level outcomes into dense step-level supervision that rewards useful actions (even in failed trajectories) while suppressing erroneous or redundant actions. Specifically, given a potentially obscure query and its corresponding ground-truth answer, ABC first performs Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery, which traces back from the answer to recover intermediate clues required to solve the question. It then applies Clue-Anchored Step Scoring to evaluate each search step against these clues, converting sparse binary outcome supervision into dense step-level rewards. Based on these rewards, we develop ABC-SFT, which reweights the loss of each turn, and ABC-GRPO, which uses the step-level scores as rewards in GRPO. Building on this framework, we train ABSeeker based on Qwen3.5-4B with only 8.5k examples. ABSeeker achieves 37.3% on BrowseComp and 39.1% on BrowseComp-ZH. With context management, the scores further improve to 55.3% and 52.9%, respectively, significantly outperforming same-scale (4B) agents and even matching the performance of larger ones (approximately 30B). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of answer-backtracked step-level credit assignment for training long-horizon search agents.

</details>


### [152] [CoPlan: A Trustworthy Co-Intelligence Interface for Care Planning through Role-Based Contestable Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05107)
*Hung Truong Thanh Nguyen,Hélène Fournier,Piper Jackson,Makoto Itoh,Shannon Freeman,Rene Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CoPlan——一个面向人类-AI协作护理规划的可争议性接口系统。它采用多智能体工作流，让专业AI智能体生成候选干预措施及支持或质疑性论点，同时人类护理规划者可以接受、拒绝、修改或添加论点后再生成最终计划。该系统在居家养老护理规划场景中进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统将推荐作为固定输出呈现，限制了利益相关者（临床医生、患者、照护者、护理团队）检查、质疑和修订计划的能力，当AI建议与临床判断、患者价值观或现实可行性冲突时缺乏灵活性。

Method: CoPlan采用多智能体工作流：专门的AI智能体生成候选干预措施以及支持性或挑战性论点；人类护理规划者可在最终计划生成前对论点进行接受、拒绝、修改或添加操作。系统支持适应性护理团队招募、基于角色的论点审查、最终护理计划生成以及通过调度智能体进行实际随访。设计上结合了"协同智能"（人类与AI互补专业能力）与"可争议性"（推荐保持可检查、可修订和可论证）。

Result: 在居家养老护理规划场景中成功演示了CoPlan系统，验证了其支持适应性护理团队招募、基于角色的论点审查、最终护理计划生成和实际后续跟进（通过调度智能体）的完整工作流。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个可争议的护理规划接口，并提供了值得信赖的人机协同护理规划的设计框架，该框架在AI辅助决策中保留了人类能动性和临床问责制。

Abstract: AI-supported care planning can help clinicians, patients, caregivers, and care teams coordinate complex decisions across clinical, functional, psychosocial, and environmental needs. However, many AI systems present recommendations as fixed outputs, limiting stakeholders' ability to inspect, challenge, and revise plans when they conflict with clinical judgment, patient values, or real-world feasibility. We present CoPlan - a Co-Intelligent and Contestable Interface for Human-AI Care Planning. CoPlan uses a multi-agent workflow in which specialized AI agents generate candidate interventions and supporting or challenging arguments, while human care planners can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before final plan generation. Through this design, CoPlan combines co-intelligence, in which humans and AI agents contribute complementary expertise, with contestability, where recommendations remain open to inspection, revision, and justification. We demonstrate CoPlan in an aging-in-place care planning scenario. The system supports adaptive care team recruitment, role-based argument review, final care plan generation, and practical follow-up through scheduling agents. This work contributes a contestable care planning interface and a design framing for trustworthy human-AI care planning that preserves human agency and clinical accountability.

</details>


### [153] [OctoLong: Mid-Training On Cross-Repository Code Contexts Enhances Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05141)
*Indraneil Paul,Falko Helm,Goran Glavaš,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OctoLong，一个通过AST解析器、语言服务器后端和包管理器递归检索代码引用的上下文工程流水线，构建百万级token的依赖丰富的代码上下文语料，并基于此训练了从600M到14B参数的OctoLong-Instruct模型套件，在长距离检索、长期状态追踪、仓库级代码理解和agentic任务上显著优于18个开源长上下文LM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文语料主要由书籍、学术文章和代码仓库构成，这些资源是有限的且往往缺乏长距离依赖关系。随着LM上下文长度急剧增加，需要依赖丰富的长上下文语料来支撑上下文学习、自我改进和长时程agentic工作流，但传统语料难以满足这一需求。

Method: 提出的OctoLong流水线结合AST解析器、语言服务器后端和包管理器，实现代码引用的递归检索，从而构建包含大量跨文件依赖关系的长代码上下文（可达百万token）。训练过程分为两步：先对基座模型（600M至14B参数）进行上下文扩展中训练，使用约500亿token的混合语料（其中约62亿token为OctoLong代码上下文），再使用约100亿token进行指令微调。

Result: 在18个SOTA开源长上下文LM上的消融实验和评估表明，仅用OctoLong数据替代传统上下文扩展语料的12%，就能在长距离检索、长期状态追踪、仓库级代码理解和下游agentic任务上取得显著提升，同时还能增强短上下文编码场景中的API使用能力。

Conclusion: OctoLong证明依赖密集的代码上下文是一种高效补充传统长上下文训练语料的手段，少量替换即可带来多方面收益，为构建高质量长上下文LMs提供了新的语料工程方向。

Abstract: Context lengths of language models (LMs) have dramatically increased, driven by the demands for in-context learning, self-improvement, and long-horizon agentic workflows. Existing long-context corpora, however, are dominated by books, academic articles, and code repositories, which are finite resources and often scarce in long-distance dependencies. In this work, we introduce OctoLong, a context engineering pipeline that instruments an AST parser, a language server backend, and a package manager to facilitate the recursive retrieval of code references, enabling the curation of dependency-rich code contexts of millions of tokens in length. We then train OctoLong-Instruct, a suite of capable long-context open LMs, derived from base models ranging in size from 600M to 14B parameters, via context-extension mid-training on a ~50B-token mixture containing ~6.2B tokens of OctoLong code contexts, followed by ~10B tokens of instruction tuning. Our training ablations and experimental evaluations against 18 state-of-the-art open-weight long-context LMs show that supplanting just 12% of traditional context-extension corpora with OctoLong data yields substantial gains in long-range retrieval, long-term state tracking, repository-level code understanding, and downstream agentic tasks, while also enhancing API usage in short-context coding scenarios.

</details>


### [154] [Argus: A General-Purpose Agentic Runtime for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05144)
*Boxiu Li,Zimo Wen,Yijia Fan,Junxiang Lei,Sufeng Guo,Jiaao Wu,Ruize Tang,Mukai Li,Yifei Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Wanbo Zhang,Runjing Gu,Yifei Gao,Yuheng Wu,Xuyao Huang,Zelong Zhao,Jiachen Zhang,Shibo Hu,Hangxi Guo,Yilin Chen,Yuzhe Zhang,Fan Yang,Chuan Wen,Xian Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Argus是一个持久的、自进化的智能体运行时系统，通过Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四个角色在持久项目状态上执行有界任务。它在固定模型权重下通过运行时状态和控制策略实现自我进化，在七个GPT-5.5基准竞技场中达到约78%的SWE-Bench Pro性能（对比Direct Copilot的59%），并展示了一致的性能提升和资源效率优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程推理要求智能体运行时能够在证据支持当前方法时保持持久执行，在测量显示失败、隐藏约束或目标设定错误时进行转向。现有方法缺乏这种自我进化的能力，无法在运行时积累经验并动态调整策略。

Method: Argus包含Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四个角色，在持久项目状态上执行有界任务。它将稳定的用户意图与操作目标、约束和验证标准分离，并通过角色拥有的评审和任务原生验证来接纳记忆、技能、程序、验证器、路由决策和拒绝的路线。模型权重保持固定，自我进化通过持久运行时状态和控制策略实现，在操作者拥有的升级点之间自主执行。

Result: 在七个GPT-5.5基准竞技场中，Argus在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%（Direct Copilot为59%），使用1.41倍聚合token。验证门控自我进化后，成熟SWE-Bench波次比启动波次少使用21%的solve-input token和15%的活动工作流时间，记录了34个验证器恢复和22个严格审查循环救援。AARRI-Bench上达到76.8%，数学数据合成上领先28.0点。优化后的RWKV6内核已合并到上游，数学战役和多论文流水线均展示了实际部署效果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，固定权重、自我进化的harness能够修订、恢复和积累验证过的方法，同时产生结构化轨迹供未来的监督学习和强化学习使用。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires an agentic runtime that can persist when evidence supports its current approach and pivot when measurements reveal failure, hidden constraints, or a misspecified objective. We present Argus, a persistent, self-evolving runtime in which Manager, Planner, Engineer, and Reviewer execute bounded missions over durable project state. Argus separates stable user intent from operational objectives, constraints, and verification criteria, and admits memories, skills, procedures, verifiers, routing decisions, and rejected routes only after role-owned review and, when available, task-native verification. Model weights remain fixed; self-evolution occurs through persistent runtime state and control policy, with autonomous execution between operator-owned escalation points. Across seven GPT-5.5 benchmark arenas, Argus achieves about 78% on SWE-Bench Pro versus 59% for Direct Copilot while using 1.41 times the aggregate tokens. After verification-gated self-evolution, mature SWE-Bench waves use 21% fewer solve-input tokens and 15% less active workflow time per task than startup waves, while recording 34 verifier recoveries and 22 strict review-loop rescues. Argus also reaches 76.8% on AARRI-Bench and a 28.0-point gap on mathematical data synthesis, with competitive GPU-kernel and language-model-training results. Beyond benchmarks, an optimized RWKV6 kernel was merged upstream; a multi-day mathematics campaign retained falsified routes and proof-backed frontier updates; and six paper pipelines completed 254 missions with 16 stage rollbacks. These results show that a fixed-weight, self-evolving harness can revise, recover, and accumulate verified approaches while producing structured trajectories for future supervised and reinforcement learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Kitchen Robotic Manipulation utilizing Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04042)
*Myung-Hwan Jeon,Sankalp Yamsani,Joohyung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化感知流水线，用于家庭操作任务中的餐具处理。该流水线集成了开放词汇目标检测、多视角分割、实例感知3D重建和2D-3D特征融合，用于6D位姿估计和抓取规划。最佳配置（LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer）在20场景厨房基准上达到89.12%的ADI，并成功部署在真实机器人上，无需环境特定重训练即可执行水池到洗碗机的转移和杯子堆叠等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在日常生活环境中部署机器人需要感知系统既鲁棒又能适应多样化的动态条件。现有系统往往缺乏灵活性，难以应对厨房等杂乱和遮挡环境下的餐具操作，因此需要一个模块化、可替换且可扩展的感知框架。

Method: 提出模块化感知流水线，包含四个核心组件：开放词汇目标检测（LLMDet）、多视角分割（SAMv2）、实例感知3D重建（GeoTransformer）以及基于DINOv2的2D-3D特征融合策略。模块化设计允许系统地替换多种视觉和几何基础模型，以便通过实验确定最优组合配置。在自定义厨房数据集上进行大规模评测，并在真实机器人上验证部署能力。

Result: 最佳配置（LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer）在包含杂乱和遮挡条件的20场景厨房基准上达到89.12%的ADI（平均距离/位姿误差指标）。真实世界演示证实该配置可直接部署于物理机器人，无需环境特定重训练，成功完成水池到洗碗机转移及杯子堆叠等任务。

Conclusion: 该模块化感知流水线验证了其适应性和可扩展性，展示了作为家庭机器人系统实用框架的潜力。其模块化设计便于后续替换更优的视觉和几何模型，为家庭机器人感知提供了灵活的基准框架。

Abstract: Deploying robots in everyday human environments requires perception systems that are both robust and adaptable to diverse, dynamic conditions. In this work, we present a modular perception pipeline for household manipulation tasks, with a focus on dishware handling in kitchen environments. The pipeline integrates open-vocabulary object detection, multi-view segmentation, instance-aware 3D reconstruction, and a 2D-3D feature fusion strategy for 6D pose estimation and grasp planning. Its modular design enables systematic substitution of multiple visual and geometric foundation models, allowing us to identify the best-performing configuration through extensive evaluation on a custom kitchen dataset. The best-performing configuration (LLMDet + SAMv2 + DINOv2 + GeoTransformer) achieves an ADI of 89.12\% on the 20-scene kitchen benchmark with cluttered and occluded conditions. Furthermore, real-world demonstrations confirm that the best configuration can be deployed on physical robots without environment-specific retraining, successfully executing tasks such as sink-to-dishwasher transfer and cup stacking. It validates the adaptability and scalability of the pipeline and highlights its potential as a practical framework for household robotic systems. Our code and supplementary materials are available at https://raivlab.github.io/FM_kitchen .

</details>


### [156] [Interpretable Fuzzy Inference for UAV Target Tracking Using Bounding-Box Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04121)
*Reza Ahmari,Ahmad Mohammadi,Vahid Hemmati,Nicholas Edmond,Hossein Z. Saghazadeh,Olusola Odeyomi,Parham Kebria,Abdollah Homaifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的模糊推理框架，用于从YOLO检测框的低维特征（质心位置、面积、宽高比）生成无人机的连续偏航角指令，无需几何建模或外部定位。该方法在VICON运动捕捉环境中的6,169个标注样本上验证，Takagi-Sugeno模型实现了0.140°的测试集平均绝对误差，且具有透明、数据高效、计算轻量等优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉引导无人机追踪地面车辆是空地协同机器人领域的重要任务，但传统的深度学习和几何重建方法存在数据需求大、依赖外部定位、建模假设复杂等问题，降低了透明度和在资源受限平台上的部署适应性。

Method: 采用可解释的模糊推理框架：首先从YOLO检测框提取三个低维特征（目标质心位置、面积、宽高比）；然后使用Mamdani模糊系统作为可解释基线（shoulder-triangle-shoulder输入分区）；再引入一阶Takagi-Sugeno模型，每个输入含三个隶属函数项，参数由训练集分位数推导，形成紧凑的27条规则结构。

Result: 在6,169个VICON标注样本上，5次随机训练-测试划分中，Takagi-Sugeno模型达到测试集MAE为0.140°±0.003°，RMSE为0.200°±0.008°，最大绝对误差为1.254°±0.121°。±1°阈值内准确率99.676%，±3°和±5°阈值内准确率100%。图像平面水平位移与预测偏航方向一致率达90.254%。

Conclusion: 该框架证明兼具透明性、数据效率、计算轻量性和实时性，适用于资源受限平台上基于视觉的无人机对移动地面目标的连续偏航引导。

Abstract: Vision-based guidance of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) toward unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) supports cooperative aerial--ground robotics, but reliable continuous yaw estimation from onboard vision remains challenging because of sensing uncertainty, limited computation, and the need for interpretable control. Existing deep-learning and geometric-reconstruction approaches often require large datasets, external localization, or complex modeling assumptions, reducing transparency and deployment suitability on resource-constrained platforms. We present an interpretable fuzzy-inference framework that generates continuous yaw commands from low-dimensional features extracted from YOLO boxes: target centroid location, area, and aspect ratio. No explicit geometric modeling is required. A Mamdani fuzzy system serves as an interpretable baseline using a shoulder--triangle--shoulder input partition. It is followed by a first-order Takagi--Sugeno model with three antecedent membership terms per input, whose parameters are derived from training-set quantiles, yielding a compact 27-rule structure. Evaluation uses 6{,}169 labeled samples from a VICON motion-capture environment. Across five randomized train--test splits, the Takagi--Sugeno model achieves a test-set mean absolute error of $0.140^\circ \pm 0.003^\circ$, a root mean squared error of $0.200^\circ \pm 0.008^\circ$, and a maximum absolute error of $1.254^\circ \pm 0.121^\circ$. Within-threshold accuracies are $99.676% \pm 0.270%$ for $\pm1^\circ$ and $100.000% \pm 0.000%$ for both $\pm3^\circ$ and $\pm5^\circ$. Directional consistency between image-plane horizontal displacement and predicted yaw sign reaches $90.254% \pm 0.612%$. These results show that the framework is transparent, data-efficient, computationally lightweight, and suitable for real-time vision-based UAV guidance toward mobile ground targets.

</details>


### [157] [SiMDex: Mining Similar Egocentric Videos for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04196)
*Nie Lin,Takehiko Ohkawa,Sijin Chen,Ruoshi Wen,Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Zhengming Zhu,Yiming Bao,Yunfei Li,Minjie Cai,Xiao Ma,Wei Xu,Yoichi Sato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SiMDex提出了一种基于相似度的数据挖掘框架，将VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型后训练中的人类数据选择问题建模为推荐问题。通过三层"召回-排序-重排"流水线，从约3200万ego-centric人类视频样本中筛选出约149万（不到总量的5%）任务相关数据，在无需改变VLA架构的前提下，将灵巧操作任务的平均成功率从47.7%提升至61.1%，显著优于同等数据量的随机采样基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来ego-centric人类视频在机器人操作领域中规模急剧增长，但并非所有人类数据都能有效帮助灵巧操作任务。现有方法盲目混合大量人类数据，缺乏对数据价值的甄别，因此如何从海量数据中选择真正有益于灵巧操作的数据成为关键问题。

Method: SiMDex将人类数据选择建模为推荐问题，采用三层流水线：1) 召回层：从3200万样本中粗筛候选；2) 排序层：依据相似度精排；3) 重排层：进一步优化最终子集。整个流程在形态无关（morphology-agnostic）的动作空间中运行，不改变VLA的架构和训练流程。

Result: 在使用相同数据量的随机采样人类数据训练的强基线（成功率47.7%）对比下，SiMDex仅使用约149万挖掘样本（不足数据池的5%）即将整体成功率提升至61.1%，证明了选择性数据策展优于不加区分的数据混合。

Conclusion: 数据质量比数据数量更重要：通过相似度驱动的选择性数据挖掘，可以显著提升VLA模型在灵巧操作上的泛化能力，为大规模人类数据在机器人操作中的有效利用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an explosive trend of scaling ego-centric human videos for robot manipulation, yet it remains unclear which data actually benefits dexterous manipulation. We present SiMDex, a similarity-based data mining framework that casts human data selection for VLA post-training in dexterous manipulation as a recommendation problem. For each robot demonstration, SiMDex employs a three-layer recall-ranking-re-ranking pipeline to extract task-relevant subsets from a pool of ~32M egocentric human samples, operating in a morphology-agnostic action space that requires no changes to VLA architecture or training. Against a strong baseline trained with an equal amount of randomly sampled human data, SiMDex uses only ~1.49M mined samples (<5% of the pool) yet improves the overall success rate from 47.7% to 61.1%, showing that selective curation outperforms indiscriminate data mixing.

</details>


### [158] [Feasibility of Embedded Photoplethysmography Sensing in Short-Duration Tactile Interactions With Pocket-Sized Robots Using IMU- and Confidence-Based Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04242)
*Turjja Datta,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将PPG传感器直接嵌入口袋大小的陪伴机器人AffectaPocket中的方案，通过两阶段滤波管道（利用机载IMU拒绝高方差片段+基于置信度的平滑算法处理恢复期）解决手持使用中的运动伪影问题，实现了自主心率监测。在26名参与者的受试者内研究中，滤波策略显著降低了平均绝对百分比误差，并与金标准腕戴式传感器达到统计学等价（p<0.05），但短时交互分析表明传感器需要较长稳定期才能收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的儿童焦虑缓解陪伴机器人依赖外部穿戴设备监测生理状态，存在可用性障碍并限制了机器人的自主性。为了在触觉交互过程中实现连续、无创的生理状态监测，需要将传感能力直接嵌入机器人本体，同时解决手持使用场景中固有的运动伪影问题。

Method: 1) 将PPG传感器直接嵌入小型陪伴机器人AffectaPocket中；2) 设计两阶段滤波管道：第一阶段利用机载IMU检测并拒绝高方差运动片段，第二阶段采用基于置信度的平滑算法处理恢复期信号；3) 采用受试者内设计（Within-Subjects Study），招募26名参与者，与常用腕戴式传感器进行对比评估。

Result: 两阶段滤波策略显著降低了心率测量的平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE），且与地面真值测量达到统计学等价（p<0.05）。短时交互分析表明，传感器需要更长的稳定期才能实现信号收敛，即短程交互中测量精度受限。

Conclusion: 嵌入机器人本体的PPG传感方案结合运动伪影抑制算法，可以在触觉交互中实现与外部腕戴设备相当的自主心率监测精度，为陪伴机器人在儿童焦虑缓解场景中的自主生理感知能力提供了可行路径；但需关注短时交互场景下的信号收敛延迟问题。

Abstract: Ubiquitous companion robots offer a promising avenue for immediate anxiety relief in children, yet their effectiveness relies on the ability to monitor physiological states continuously and unobtrusively. Current solutions often depend on external wearables, which impose usability barriers and limit the robot's autonomy. This paper investigates the integration of an embedded photoplethysmography (PPG) sensor directly into a pocket-sized companion robot, AffectaPocket, to enable self-contained heart rate monitoring during tactile interaction. We address the significant challenge of motion artifacts inherent in handheld usage by implementing a two-stage filtering pipeline that utilizes an onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) to reject high-variance segments and a confidence-based smoothing algorithm for recovery periods. We evaluated the system against a commonly used wrist worn sensor in a Within-Subjects Study with 26 participants. Our results demonstrate that the filtering strategy significantly reduced the Mean Absolute Percentage Error and achieved statistical equivalence to the ground truth measurements (p<0.05). Analysis of short-duration interactions shows that the sensor requires stability over longer periods to converge.

</details>


### [159] [SAFECAST: Robust Failure Detection for VLA Policies with Contrast-Set Training and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04246)
*Harshitha Rajaprakash,Aditeya Prajapati,Rong Xue,Abrar Anwar,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFECAST利用对比集扰动来改进隐藏状态风险探针的训练和校准，使其在部署时分布偏移（如杂乱场景、干扰物、光照变化、新物体、初始状态改变和指令改写）下能更可靠地检测视觉-语言-动作策略的滚动失败，在DROID真实世界和LIBERO仿真实验中显著提升了失败检测的ROC-AUC分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略在部署时常常遭遇分布偏移（如杂乱场景、干扰物、光照变化、新物体、初始状态改变和指令改写），导致策略失败。已有的基于隐藏状态的风险探针结合功能共形预测方法虽然可以检测滚动失败，但其可靠性依赖于校准数据与部署条件的匹配程度，在实际部署中这一条件难以满足。

Method: SAFECAST的核心思想是利用对比集扰动（contrast set perturbations）来改进隐藏状态探针的训练和校准。具体而言：(1) 通过视觉和语言两种模态的对比集扰动来增强训练数据的多样性，使探针更好地泛化到部署时的分布偏移；(2) 利用扰动后的数据进行校准，改善功能共形预测的可靠性；(3) 在多个VLM主干网络和真实/仿真环境中验证效果。

Result: SAFECAST在真实世界DROID和LIBERO仿真实验中，使用多种VLM主干网络，均比现有最先进基线显著提升了失败检测的ROC-AUC分数。研究发现：(1) 同时使用视觉和语言对比集扰动进行数据增强时收益最大；(2) 使用对比集扰动时，仿真到真实的校准比仅使用真实滚动数据能产生更好的探针。

Conclusion: 对比集扰动是一种有效的策略失败检测改进手段，能够缓解校准数据与部署条件不匹配的问题。视觉和语言双模态的扰动联合使用效果最佳，且对比集扰动还能提升仿真到真实迁移的校准效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often fail under deployment-time distribution shifts such as clutter, distractor objects, lighting changes, novel objects, altered initial states, and reworded instructions. Hidden-state-based risk probes combined with functional conformal prediction can detect rollout failures, but their reliability depends on calibration data matching deployment conditions. We introduce SAFECAST, which leverages contrast set perturbations to improve hidden-state probe training and calibration for deployment time shift. SAFECAST statistically significantly improves failure detection ROC-AUC scores over a state of the art baseline in both real-world DROID and LIBERO simulation experiments across multiple VLM backbones. We further find that SAFECAST benefits most when both visual and language contrast set perturbations are used to augment data, and that with contrast set perturbations, sim-to-real calibration leads to better probes than using real rollout data only.

</details>


### [160] [Structured LLM Reasoning for Zero-Shot Human--Robot Coordination Under Hidden Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04309)
*Dong Hae Mangalindan,Anand Gokhale,Francesco Bullo,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化大语言模型（LLM）的零样本人机协作架构，用于具有私有目标视图的合作建造任务。该架构以Dec-POMDP为理论基础，将决策分解为五个模块（ToM推理、分层规划、对话理解、动作验证和反馈重规划），在人类参与实验中相较于无ToM推理的消融基线和多智能体强化学习基线，所需交互步骤更少且信任评分更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零样本人机协作场景中，传统方法（如多智能体强化学习）需要大量离线训练且缺乏对私有目标视图下人机协调的泛化能力；而直接使用LLM进行决策又难以保证物理可行性。作者希望系统性地分解团队决策问题，利用LLM处理棘手的推理与规划计算，同时保留传统验证机制确保物理可行性，以提升任务协调效率和人类体验。

Method: 受Dec-POMDP（去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程）框架指导，作者构建了结构化LLM架构，将决策过程分解为五个模块：(i) 动作条件的心理理论（Theory-of-Mind）推理，用于推断人类伙伴的意图与目标；(ii) 分层规划；(iii) 对话理解；(iv) 动作验证（物理可行性校验）；(v) 基于反馈的重规划。该架构以LLM作为不可计算推理和规划的可处理替代模型，同时保留传统验证环节。

Result: 在人类参与者实验中，该方法的交互步骤数少于无ToM推理的消融基线以及离线训练的多智能体强化学习策略；同时，参与者在交互后给出的信任评分也高于两个基线。这表明结构化分解+LLM推理+传统验证的组合在任务协调与用户体验两方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 系统性分解团队决策问题、以LLM作为不可计算推理与规划计算的可处理替代，以及保留传统验证保障物理可行性，能够同时改善任务协调效率和人类体验。该工作为LLM在零样本人机协作中的应用提供了一个有前景的架构范式。

Abstract: We present a structured large-language-model (LLM) architecture for zero-shot human--robot coordination in a cooperative construction task with private goal views. Guided by a Dec-POMDP formulation, the architecture decomposes decision-making into (i) action-conditioned Theory-of-Mind (ToM) inference, (ii) hierarchical planning, (iii) conversation interpretation, (iv) action verification, and (v) feedback-based replanning. We compare the proposed method with an ablation without ToM inference and a multi-agent reinforcement-learning policy trained offline over many goal pairs. In human-participant experiments, the proposed method required fewer interaction steps and yielded higher post-interaction trust ratings than both baselines. These results suggest that systematically decomposing the team decision problem, using LLMs as tractable surrogates for otherwise intractable inference and planning computations, and retaining conventional verification for physical feasibility can improve both task coordination and the human experience.

</details>


### [161] [Design and Flight of an Ion-propelled Micro Hovercraft Leveraging Ground Proximity Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04343)
*C. Luke Nelson,Grant Nations,Mrinmoy Modak,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了首个离子推进的微型气垫船，利用地面效应将电空气动力学推力效率提升至16 mN/W，优于同尺寸电空气动力机器人的一个数量级，为新型静音、固态推进的微型飞行机器人开辟了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电空气动力学推进具有静音和固态特性，适合微型飞行器，但其有限的效率一直阻碍着动力自主飞行。近年研究发现靠近地面时小型离子推进器的推力密度和效率可大幅提升，本文旨在利用这一地面效应探索厘米级气垫船的设计空间。

Method: 首先进行实证研究，表征不同几何形状和配置的被动式气垫船裙边对性能的收益与权衡，然后基于实验结果制造可行的点设计方案，搭建手掌大小的离子推进气垫船样机。

Result: 演示了手掌大小的气垫船，可长时间飞行、承受数十次起降循环、被动稳定以抵抗显著机械扰动，且几乎零可闻噪声。测量到的推力效率为16 mN/W，额外有效载荷约1.5克（自身质量约1.6克），优于任何同尺寸电空气动力推进机器人一个数量级。

Conclusion: 这是开放文献中首次展示离子推进的微型气垫船，为整类新型机器人指明了方向，有望实现低空飞行的新型静音无人机平台。

Abstract: Electroaerodynamic propulsion is compelling for use in micro air vehicles due to its silent and solid-state nature, but its limited efficiency has thus far precluded a path towards power-autonomous flight. Recent work has shown that thrust density and efficiency for small-scale atmospheric ion thrusters can be vastly increased when operating close to a ground plane. Here, we explore the design space of centimeter-scale hovercraft, which can leverage this ground effect for low-altitude flight. We first perform an empirical investigation, characterizing the performance benefits and trade-offs for different geometries and configurations of passive hovercraft skirts, then use the results to fabricate a viable point design. We demonstrate a palm-sized hovercraft that, while tethered to an external power source, can fly for extended periods, withstand dozens of takeoff and landing cycles, passively stabilize to reject significant mechanical disturbances, and generate practically zero audible noise signature. The measured thrust efficiency of 16 mN/W and additional payload capacity of almost 1.5 grams above the vehicle's self mass of about 1.6 grams exceeds any similarly sized electroaerodynamically propelled robot by an order of magnitude. This is the first time an ion-propelled micro hovercraft has been shown in the open literature, and our work points the way towards an entirely new class of robot.

</details>


### [162] [Approximate Multi-Objective Search Under Rulebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04398)
*Omar Muhammetkulyyev,Oren Salzman,Tichakorn Wongpiromsarn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RA*pex算法，用于高效计算规则书（rulebook）下的ε-近似最优解集。该算法引入ε-rule-dominance概念，结合维度缩减和分层封闭集维护，在保证每个规则书最优解都被返回集中至少一个解所ε-rule支配的前提下，计算速度比现有方法快两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人规划常常涉及多个具有复杂优先级关系的目标（如安全、效率、法规合规）。规则书形式化了这些优先级关系，支持目标的偏序排序，但计算完整的规则书最优解集计算成本极高，亟需高效的近似求解方法。

Method: 1) 引入ε-rule-dominance概念，作为规则书下近似支配的规范化定义；2) 提出RA*pex最佳优先搜索算法，利用维度缩减加速多目标搜索，同时通过维护分离的封闭集和基于截断/剩余规则集的支配检查来尊重规则层级结构；3) 提供形式化分析证明算法性质。

Result: 理论证明每个规则书最优解都被返回集中至少一个解ε-rule支配；实验结果表明计算时间比现有方法快两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: RA*pex在保证理论保证的前提下显著提升了规则书下多目标机器人规划的计算效率，为处理复杂优先级关系的规划问题提供了实用的近似求解方案。

Abstract: Robotic planning often involves multiple objectives with complex priority relationships, such as safety, efficiency, and regulatory compliance. Rulebooks formalize these relationships, allowing partial ordering of objectives that generalizes both Pareto and lexicographic dominance. Computing the full set of rulebook-optimal solutions, however, is computationally expensive.
